# 📖 THE GOLD ROBOT HOLY BOOK
*The one book of the whole company. Born 2026-10-17 by owner's order. Chapter 1 = who works here (with 5-year-old stories, inner parts, and dictionaries). Chapter 2 = the history, short and informative. Chapter 3 = the future.*

## The Book's own laws (permanent — keep always)

1. **ONE BOOK, ONE TRUTH** — every significant event is stamped HERE (a short Chapter 2 line + update the affected Chapter 1/3 entry). No other book exists.
2. **THE EVENING RHYTHM** — after each paper: update the Chapter 1 cards (doorman, judges, crowd) + add one Chapter 2 line. Five minutes a night.
3. **DATE-STAMPED CARDS** — every "Now:" line carries its as-of date, so "current" is always anchored.
4. **THE COMPRESSION & DOUBLE-CAPACITY RULE (expanded by owner's order, 2026-10-31)** — expanded from `1,000` to **`< 2,000` lines** ("double size") so vital Judge Cardiology formulas, institutional book/paper citations, and multi-day raw-replay receipts are preserved in full detail; when Chapter 2 grows large, only old routine daily logs compress to one line per day while core medical/engineering dossiers stay intact forever.
5. **THE MULTI-DAY (`3–5–10` DAY) BRAIN & STRATEGY EVIDENCE LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-29)** — before changing or updating any Brain weakness or Strategy rule (Judge weights/cuts, `POWER` regime/force gates, `Shooting` filters, `Escort`/`PositionManager` exits), **ALWAYS test the candidate update against the previous `3–5–10` days of recorded data** (covering both Trend days and Flat/Range days) and inspect whether it hurts any regime. Promote to **LIVE** only after multi-day data proves net improvement without breaking another regime. **Whenever multi-day historical testing is not possible or shows regime conflict / small sample size, put the rule in SHADOW MODE (`SHADOW_ONLY`, live gate `0`) for `3–5+` live days first** and grade its counterfactuals in nightly `X-Ray` before switching live.
6. **THE 5-YEAR-OLD OWNER STORY LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-30)** — after every update, report, audit, or research pass — and **ALWAYS BEFORE** making or installing a change — tell the owner everything discovered as a clear, vivid, **informative story for a 5-year-old owner** (naming the real organs, real numbers/receipts, and exact causes/effects in simple everyday pictures) so the owner understands the full situation before deciding or applying any change.
7. **THE CONTINUOUS TRUSTWORTHY RESEARCH & INSTITUTIONAL STUDY LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-30)** — always keep learning, studying, and digging deep into **reliable, trustworthy primary sources** (peer-reviewed academic papers, regulated bank & institutional trading standards such as BIS / MiFID II RTS 6 / SEC 15c3-5 / FIA / CME rulebooks, and official vendor/SDK engineering documentation) on every subject and organ we touch, comparing our design against real institutional standards and recording the citations and takeaways in this Book.
8. **THE RAW-DATA-ONLY CURRENT-VERSION REPLAY LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-31)** — because we update and fix the app continuously, historical decisions, ledgers, and reports generated by **previous** app versions reflect old bugs, old weights, and retired judges, and are **NOT trustworthy** for judging our current system. Whenever studying how our app works or diagnosing its strengths and weaknesses across past days, **ALWAYS feed past days' RAW market data (`ticks_*.csv[.gz]` + `mbo_*.csv[.gz]`) directly into the LATEST app version (`tools/raw_replay.py`)** with strict chronological causality (zero look-ahead) so every organ, panel vote, gate, and exit is measured on how our **current** version actually reacts to real scenarios.
9. **THE CAUSAL ISOLATION & ORDERED STAGE TESTING LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-31 — CHECK BEFORE EVERY UPDATE)** — before planning or applying any multi-part update, **ALWAYS map how subsystems connect and affect each other's tests (`Stage 1: Measurement/Speed/Timeframe Ruler -> Stage 2: Judges/Signal Panel -> Stage 3: Entry/Session/Confidence Gates -> Stage 4: Exit/Trails/Stops`)** and execute + test updates **one isolated stage at a time**. Never mix ruler changes, signal changes, entry-gate changes, and exit changes in one commit; otherwise downstream metrics shift simultaneously and obscure what truly helped or hurt.
10. **THE HEART-SURGEON / JUDGE CARDIOLOGY LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-31)** — **our Judges Panel is the Heart of our robot body.** Never recklessly delete, blindfold, or amputate an active judge from a noisy single net score. Always perform a wise **Cardiologist Health Checkup** first: (a) read every judge's exact Python code & math line-by-line; (b) separate **pure directional eyesight (`RIGHT%` and `GROSS_PTS` before spread)** from **standalone spread tax (`$0.50 × over-firing`)** and **regime tail risk (`TREND` freight-train crashes vs `RANGE` wins)**; (c) inspect `1..4`-bar active-`TIMEFRAME` path physics (`FOLLOW`, `SHAKE->W`, `TRAP->L`); and (d) heal wiring/threshold/regime-seatbelt defects with gentle, reversible care verified on multi-day raw replay (`Laws #5, #8, #9`) or shadow mode first.
11. **THE UNIVERSAL CHART TIMEFRAME FITNESS LAW (owner's permanent law, 2026-10-31)** — **every section, judge, technical indicator, order-flow window, multi-timeframe confirmation ladder, bar-service cache, and audit/replay grading clock across the entire app MUST derive from and dynamically fit the active chart `TIMEFRAME` (`M3` = `3` minutes today, and any `M1..H1` timeframe in the future) with zero leftover `M1`/`M5` calculation mismatches.** Pure session/wall-clock profiles (`VWAP`, `POC`, `Value Area`, `H1=60m` / `H4=240m` `htf_poc`) stay anchored to session `M1` volume distribution, while all chart-bar organs (`ATR`, `ADX -> regime`, `BB/RSI/MACD/MA`, `volume_roc`, `sweep`, `cvd_divergence`, `order_blocks`, Classroom `A1` bar caching, `POWER`, `ORDER_FLOW_WINDOW_SECONDS`, strictly-higher `MTF_LADDER` multiples, and `judge_clock = JUDGE_HORIZON_BARS × tf_min`) scale automatically whenever `config.TIMEFRAME` changes.

**The house laws (never dropped):** NEVER GUESS — every claim carries its receipt · one change per week · never loosen thresholds to force trades · **3–5–10 day multi-day replay before live Brain/Strategy changes, else shadow-first (`2026-10-29`)** · **5-year-old informative owner story after every report/research & before every change (`2026-10-30`)** · **always study reliable/trustworthy institutional & primary sources (`2026-10-30`)** · **always evaluate past days by replaying RAW market data (`ticks` + `mbo`) through the LATEST app version — never trust pre-fix historical decisions/reports (`2026-10-31`)** · **always map subsystem causal order and build/test one isolated stage at a time (`Law #9`, `2026-10-31`)** · **treat Judges as the Heart of the robot body — wise Cardiology checkups (`GROSS` vs `SPREAD` vs `REGIME` + line-by-line code & books) before any surgical tune-up (`Law #10`, `2026-10-31`)** · **every section & judge must always fit and dynamically scale with the active chart `TIMEFRAME` (`Law #11`, `2026-10-31`)** · design review before installs · paper-first, `ALLOW_LIVE_TRADING=0` · fix at source · user pushes to GitHub, git bash only · backups never staged · `.env` is owner-managed (depth is 150, not 250 — owner's hand, 10-13) · **STRUCTURE FROM STANDARDS, NUMBERS FROM RECEIPTS** · **THE FOUR-PASS AUDIT (owner's law, 10-17, born from the fusion-v1 audit — always the same test, for every change made to the app from now on): ① read every installed line via the actual diff ② census every caller of the touched functions across the whole repo ③ reverse-engineering tests — corners and each function ④ a function-by-function verification report** · **THE BOOK IS UPDATED AT EVERY CHECK-IN (owner's standing order, 10-19: "always and always"; # CHAPTER 1 — THE MINDMAP: WHO WORKS HERE

## How a decision travels (source design; latest owner receipt 2026-10-23)

```
STEP 1 THE FEED      BookMap -> ticks.csv + mbo.csv (GoldBridge) -> prices, prints, order events
      |              guards: quote/L2/instrument freshness + safety receipt; 10-23 owner run fresh at acquisition, but bridge receipt time ≠ exchange latency
STEP 2 THE BRAIN     Judges panel -> Scout doors -> POWER v2 (POWER_BAR_SECONDS; M5 source default) -> Shooting plan
      |              guards: session/news, A2 market trigger, Crowd v2, quote/geometry/queue checks
STEP 3 THE DOORMAN   Configured AI/model + rule fallbacks; keeps actual model label and separate decision source
      |              AI may veto/advise; it cannot lower Step 4's owner-configured confidence threshold
STEP 4 THE EXECUTOR  CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD (source default 70; owner `.env`=35; loop logs Step-4 threshold 35) -> configured corridor
      |              guards: mode, trading switch, risk/caps; live-account path must remain blocked
STEP 5 THE RECORDER  Diary, decisions log, snapshots, shadow-only candidates (receipts; no replay in live cycle)
```

The owner reports current local `.env` values `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD=35`, A2 cap `100 fires/day`, and `ALLOW_LIVE_TRADING=0` (2026-10-22), and confirms the MT5 terminal is DEMO. In the owner-pasted 11:44–11:50 Budapest loop logs, Step 4 prints threshold 35, A2 prints `enabled=True fires 0/100`, and the Shooting receipt prints `allow_live=False`; these confirm displayed process values in that excerpt, not the whole `.env` or exact source SHA. No order/open position appears. Demo orders remain possible because `TRADING_ENABLED=True` if all gates pass. The A2 cap counts trigger fires, not orders or fills. `TIMEFRAME=M3` is logged; the fixed-M5 probe has zero usable rows while the active M3 tail is full. Never infer other runtime settings from `.env.example`. `TIMEFRAME` drives POWER's active bar length (`POWER_BAR_SECONDS`); the source default is M5, while M3 applies only when owner configuration selects it. The available startup probe is fixed M5 and is refused at a non-M5 active interval; the classifier then warms from contiguous live bars. Step 2 Shooting plans are analysis-only; paper simulation is not an order. **Historical source warning (SHA `70ba1bc`; superseded by later reviewed source):** at that snapshot, spread telemetry and L2/quote alignment were unresolved. The later `shadow-only-review` source at `4f71043` validates level timestamps, instrument identity, crossed-book/quote/last alignment, and emits measured spread. In the owner's 2026-10-23 cycle it logged spread `0.0074%`, quote valid, and L2 validated at acquisition. During later analysis the snapshot reported `available_continuity_reset` after a source/instrument continuity reset; treat continuity as reset, not as a bad quote. **Provenance limit remains:** BookMap timestamps are bridge-receipt times; exchange-event/end-to-end feed latency is not verified.

**The 9 doors every trade must pass** (architecture map audited 2026-10-21; runtime sample added 2026-10-23): 1. 🛰️ FEED fresh · 2. 🕐 SESSION + warm-up · 3. 💪 POWER activity/dominance/family gates · 4. ⚖️ BELIEF meets the owner-configured `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD` (source default 70; owner `.env`=35 and loop logs Step-4 threshold 35); an existing Power carry remains at that same configured threshold, while below-threshold Power candidates are shadow-only and non-executable · 5. 🚪 Step-3 decision, POWER direction and Shooting plan remain consistent (an opposing AI action vetoes; a quiet AI yields only to the configured-threshold carry) · 6. 👁️ A2 trigger is required for aggressive market entry (source/example default off; 11:44–11:50 runtime reports `enabled=True`, zero fires) · 7. 👥 CROWD v2 Three Fair Questions · 8. 🎯 QUALITY geometry, reward/R:R, cost, route/queue · 9. 🛡️ RISK, execution mode and independent executor guards. Passive route eligibility never overrides `LIMIT_ORDER_ENABLED`; when policy-disabled it can be recorded only as a shadow candidate. `ALLOW_LIVE_TRADING=0` remains the standing safety policy; the runtime receipt says `allow_live=False`, owner confirms DEMO, and no order appears in the excerpt.

## COMPANY 1 — THE FACTORY (the trading robot)

### 🛰️ 1. The Feed Reader (STEP 1)
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the robot's eyes and ears. It listens to the market all day and writes down every price, every trade, and every order — and if the market goes quiet, it tells everyone "I can't see!" instead of guessing.

**📦 Inside (5 parts):**
1. **The Bridge Reader** — 🧸 reads the two notebooks BookMap writes. *Technical: parses ticks.csv (trade prints: price/volume/side/time) + mbo.csv (order-by-order events).*
2. **GoldBridge** — 🧸 the pipe from BookMap to the notebooks; if it unticks, the pipe freezes. *Technical: the .jar addon; re-tick = revive (the O23 lesson).*
3. **The Freshness Guard** — 🧸 the stopwatch; if the last price is too old, everything stops safely. *Technical: feed age → STALE_FEED, all organs fail-closed.*
4. **The Tick Window** — 🧸 keeps only the most recent pages so the robot isn't slow. *Technical: `tick_data[-5000:]` rolling buffer.*
5. **The Duplicate Catcher** — 🧸 throws away photocopies so nothing is counted twice. *Technical: dedupe; ~3% exact duplicates dropped daily.*

*Now (as of 2026-11-09, `Stage B1` + `Stage B1b` / `Section #10` SEALED `10/14`):* Live `xray_2026-11-06.txt` (`2.9h`, `23,832` prints, `1,580,515` MBO lines = `~151/s`) passed `TEST 3 PIPELINE` & `TEST 4 DATA QUALITY` (`STEP1 = 111ms`), while exposing 5 live bridge bottlenecks cured in **`Stage B1` (`134e864`)** + **`Stage B1b` (`3a00651`, verified & SEALED on `replay-28` `2,998` cycles)**: ① **Windows `WinError 32` safe file rotation** (`saved_pos` seek-restore + `60s` retry cooldown + `<=30ms` lock hold in `_BridgeTail` & `_MboTail`); ② **Law #11 time-windowed MBO retention + *Cont, Kukanov & Stoikov (2014)* near-corridor (`±$8.00`) priority selector** (`replay-28` `[8i]`: `Active_Cyc=2998/2998`, `Avg_Span=42.7s`, `Near_Corridor_Share=100.0%` `5,993,601/5,996,000`, boosting `[8e]` institutional iceberg visibility **`6.2x` per cycle** `550` cyc at `58.8%` `iceberg[fix]` WR `+2.147G/call`); ③ **numeric UTC epoch sort + cross-format MBO contract matching** (`GCZ6.COMEX@RITHMIC <-> GCZ6`); ④ **dynamic `config.BOOKMAP_MAX_DEPTH_LEVELS` (`150`) + zero-reparse `fast_m1_candles`**; and ⑤ **`[8i]` + `Stage B1b` Replay Stream Fidelity**, eliminating `519 -> 0` (`18.0% -> 0.0%`, `valid=2,998/2,998 = 100.0%`) offline `crossed_book` cycles by preserving intra-file same-ms `Remove -> Update -> Remove` transitions & institutional split `Last` prints. *Provenance limit remains:* timestamps are bridge-receipt time, not CME Aurora exchange-event time.

### 🧠 2. The Brain Room (STEP 2) — overview
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the thinking room. Many little helpers each look at the market their own way and shout their opinion; then the strong man (POWER) decides if there's really a wind, and the architect (Shooting) draws the plan.

**📦 Inside (11 parts — each is its own section below):** ① the Judges Panel (**`2026-11-02` Sealed `Stage 2R-b` Roster:** `9` active `100%` gross-positive voters [`7` tape/book/hidden/map + `2` live `WORLD`] + `2` dormant `_ghost_on` + `23` retired in `config.RETIRED_JUDGES` = `34` cataloged; retired judges still write shadow notes) ② Scout ③ POWER v2 ④ Shooting (+ Crowd v2 + `Stage 3d` Sniper + `Stage 4c` Pebble Corridor) ⑤ the Paper Simulator ⑥ Escort (`Stage 4b` `1.5x ATR` Chandelier Wave Trail) ⑦ A2 Three Eyes (`Stage 4a` `max(1.80, 1.0xATR)` floor) ⑧ the L3 Order-Book Analyzer (`Stage 3R` Unplugged-MBO Guard) ⑨ the News Calendar ⑩ the Volatility Ruler (ATR) ⑪ the Warm-Up Controller + Local Active-TF (`M3`) Regime Bar Cache.

### ⚖️ 3. The Judges Panel — THE HEART OF THE ROBOT BODY (Cardiologist Code, Math & Textbook Dossier — `2026-10-31` Baseline + `2026-11-02 Stage 2R-b` Sealed Roster)

#### C. Line-by-Line Code, Formula & Institutional Textbook Dossier for Every Active Judge (`step2_market_analysis.py`)

1. **`mtf` (`L3100–3118`, `_derive_mtf_from_m1` at `L4405–4421`, `_detect_tf_trend` at `L4369–4386`):**
   - **Code & Math:** Resamples `M1` candles into `M5`, `M15`, and `H1` (`>=20` bars each); each timeframe checks `EMA(9) > EMA(20) * 1.0001 -> UP` / `< 0.9999 -> DOWN`, casting up to three stacked votes (`H1=1.0`, `M15=0.8`, `M5=0.6` when `V6_4TEAMS_ENABLED=false`; parsed as `mtf` in `judge_panel.py`).
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Bouchaud et al. 2018* Ch. 2 metaorder splitting; *Dalton 2013* higher-timeframe control):** Institutional TWAP/VWAP metaorders persist across `M15–H1` horizons, giving `mtf` **`+3,560.8` gross / `+2,494.3` net pts** (`#1` judge). Never alter its EMA math; in `judge_panel` / `SignalEngine`, ensure its weight reflects its `#1` edge vs noisy short-clock judges.
2. **`vwap_trend` (`L3620–3630`):**
   - **Code & Math:** `if regime == "TREND":` votes `+1` (`0.5 * vwap_mult`) when `price > vwap and closes[-1] > closes[-2]`, `-1` when `price < vwap and closes[-1] < closes[-2]`.
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Berkowitz, Logue & Noser 1988*; *Cartea et al. 2015*):** `L3625`'s `regime == "TREND"` gate is our model seatbelt (`+436.0` net pts). Adding a gentle non- opposing VWAP slope check (`vwap_now >= vwap_5m_ago` for `BUY`) makes it even cleaner at trend rollovers.
3. **`value_area` (`L3640–3671`):**
   - **Code & Math:** Computes `70%` session volume Value Area (`VAH`, `VAL`) and `atr_v`. Currently has **zero `regime` check**: `0 < (vah - price) <= atr_v -> SELL (0.7*vwap_mult)` (`near_vah`), `0 < (price - val) <= atr_v -> BUY (0.7*vwap_mult)` (`near_val`), `price > vah -> BUY (0.5*vwap_mult)`, `price < val -> SELL (0.5*vwap_mult)`.
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Steidlmayer 1986*; *Dalton 2013* Initiative vs Responsive & 80% Rule):** Already earns **`+1,250.3` gross / `+344.8` net**, yet leaks points because `near_vah` fades pullbacks inside `TREND` rivers while `price > vah` chases 1-tick pokes outside `VAH` on `RANGE` lakes! Conditioning `near_vah/near_val` fade to `regime != "TREND"` and `price > vah / < val` breakout to `regime == "TREND"` (or 2-bar acceptance outside VA) aligns `L3655–3671` 100% with Dalton.
4. **`spoof_invert` (`L3482–3503`) & `l3_aggr_limit` (`L3264–3276`):**
   - **Code & Math:** `spoof_invert` counts `spoof_levels` (`size >= 100` canceled within `<= 2.0s`) below vs above `price`: fake bids only -> `SELL`, fake asks only -> `BUY` (`54.3%` right, `+0.816/c` net). `l3_aggr_limit` checks `aggressive_buy_volume / total_aggr > 0.60` with `vol > 100` -> `BUY` (`< 0.40` -> `SELL`) (`61.5%` right — highest accuracy in the robot!).
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Cartea et al. 2015* Ch. 12; *Cont, Kukanov & Stoikov 2014*; CME Rule 575):** Both are textbook-pure L3 signals. **Do not touch their core logic.**
5. **`absorption` (`L1364–1468, L3207–3212` — Heart Group 2: `+662.8` Gross Alpha Hidden Under Spread Chatter):**
   - **Code & Math:** `_update_absorption` records `+1` (bid wall `>= wall_size` within `$0.40` of best bid shrinks by `>= 25%` while `best_bid >= lvl_p - 0.05`, or top bid shrinks `>= 2` consecutive snapshots) and `-1` (ask wall) over a `180s` rolling list; `SignalEngine` at `L3209` votes on **`if _abs_net:` (`abs(bid_cnt - ask_cnt) >= 1`)**!
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Frey & Sandås 2009*; *Cont & Kukanov 2017*):** `U-05` fixed the sign (`51.8%` right, **`+662.8` gross pts**!), proving passive wall defense is real! Three gentle leaks remain: (a) `_abs_net == ±1` fires on `66.1%` of cycles (`2,137/3,233`); (b) L2 size shrinkage (`lvl_s < was`) does not verify that aggressive prints actually traded at `lvl_p` (so partial L2 cancels mimic absorption); and (c) `_prune_absorption` (`L1374`) keeps `+1` bid-absorption events alive for `180s` even after price breaks `-$2` *below* that absorbed bid wall! **Wise Tune-Up:** Prune broken-level events (`price < lvl_p - 0.20` invalidates bid absorption; `price > lvl_p + 0.20` invalidates ask absorption) and require `abs(_abs_net) >= 2` with trade-print confirmation near touch.
6. **`footprint_levels` (`L1669–1676, L3226–3234`) & `footprint_delta` (`L3219–3225`) — Heart Group 2 (`+426.4` & `+265.4` Gross Alpha):**
   - **Code & Math:** In `FootprintBuilder.build_footprint` (`L1673`), **`if vols["buy"] > vols["sell"]: buying_levels.append(price)`** — horizontal comparison with **zero ratio and zero lot floor (`1 > 0` counts!)**; `L3228` votes when `len(buying_levels) > len(selling_levels) * 1.3` (`1,905` calls). `footprint_delta` (`L3221`) votes at `w=1.0` whenever `abs(delta_imbalance) >= 0.08` (`54%/46%` split, `1,666` calls) with **zero check on bar price displacement**.
   - **Books & Diagnosis (*Easley, López de Prado & O'Hara 2012 VPIN*; Institutional Diagonal Footprint "Effort vs Result"):** Both have **positive gross alpha (`+426.4` & `+265.4`)**, proving `B5` (`180s` slice) healed morning contamination! To turn positive gross into positive **net**: (a) `footprint_levels` should count genuine volume-backed diagonal/level imbalances (`>= 2.5x` ratio & `>= 5` lots, or stacked `>= 3` levels) held on the right side of `price`; (b) `footprint_delta` should raise deadband `0.08 -> 0.18`, lower `w` (`1.0 -> 0.6`), and require **Effort-vs-Result (`Kyle's λ > 0`)** — i.e., positive `180s` delta must not close near the bar's low (which signals passive sell absorption, not buyer control!).
7. **`cvd_momentum` (`L3834–3843`) & `l3_net_flow` (`L3396–3410`) — Heart Group 2:**
   - **Code & Math:** `cvd_momentum` votes `0.5` on `order_flow.delta >= 15 and buying_pressure >= 54%` (`50.5%` right, `+143.9` gross, `1,094` calls). `l3_net_flow` votes `1.0` (`2.0` in `TREND`) on `abs(aggr_buy_vol - aggr_sell_vol) >= 100` (`55.6%` right, `3/5` pos days, `45` calls).
   - **Wise Tune-Up:** `l3_net_flow` is already selective and accurate (`55.6%`); for `cvd_momentum`, requiring `180s` price displacement in the direction of `delta` (`>= +0.10 * ATR`) filters absorbed delta chop.
8. **`vwap_bands` (`L3678–3716`) & `cvd_divergence` (`L3546–3553, L5360–5372`) & `l3_imbalance` (`L3238–3263`) — Heart Group 3 (High-`RIGHT%` Range Valves Missing `TREND` Seatbelts):**
   - **Exact Code Proof:**
     - In **`vwap_bands`**, `L3702` (`±1σ`) has **`elif price >= band_1u and regime == "RANGE":`**, **but `L3678` (`±2.5σ`, `w=1.8 * vwap_mult`!) and `L3690` (`±2.0σ`, `w=1.3 * vwap_mult`!) OMIT `regime == "RANGE"`!** On `10-30`'s `-115.3 pt` waterfall (`ADX > 35`), Gold rode `-2σ` and `-2.5σ` downward all afternoon while `vwap_bands` screamed **`+1.0 BUY` at `w=1.44–1.80`**, overpowering `mtf` (`0.50`) + `vwap_trend` (`0.60`)! Yet across `11` days (`10-16..30`), `vwap_bands` has **`53.7%` RIGHT%** and **`+249.6` net pts** because it dominates Range days!
     - In **`cvd_divergence`**, `L5368–5371` emits `+1.0 BUY` when `price_roc < -0.001 and cvd_15 > 50` and `L3546` applies `SIGNAL_W_DIVERGENCE=1.2` (`0.60` parsed) **with zero `regime` check**! It won **`3/5` days** (`10-26, 10-28, 10-29` Range/Mixed), and lost `-804.9` net SOLELY because during `10-27` / `10-30` trends, retail traders aggressively market-bought (`cvd_15 > +50`) into falling prices (`price_roc < -0.001` = **bearish institutional limit absorption**, *Easley et al. 2012*)!
     - In **`l3_imbalance`**, `L3238–3263` scores **`54.1%` RIGHT%** on `imb_w >= 0.12` (`w=0.80`), winning calm bars but getting steamrolled when `TREND` momentum eats static resting depth (*Bouchaud 2018* latent liquidity).
   - **Wise Tune-Up (Never Delete `53.7%–54.1%` Right Judges — Buckle Their `TREND` Seatbelt!):**
     1. In `vwap_bands` (`L3678–3700`): gate `±2.0σ` out of `regime == "TREND"`, and allow `±2.5σ` in `TREND` **only** on a confirmed climax rejection bar (bar closes back inside band with `CLV >= 0.65` for `BUY` / `<= 0.35` for `SELL`).
     2. In `cvd_divergence` (`L3546`): silence counter-trend divergence when `regime == "TREND"` unless price structure confirms exhaustion (higher M3 low / bullish close for `+1`, lower M3 high / bearish close for `-1`).
     3. In `l3_imbalance`: trim `w` `0.80 -> 0.45` and suppress counter-`TREND` static depth votes when `l3_aggr_limit` or `l3_net_flow` sweeps against the resting book.
9. **`l3_large_ofi` (`L1758–1768, L3383–3393, L5586–5595`), `iceberg` (`L3412–3480`), & `volume_roc` (`L2485–2489, L3721–3731`) — Heart Group 4 (3 Confirmed Code/Wiring Bugs):**
   - **Bug #1 — `l3_large_ofi` `_ref_mid == 0.0` Call-Order Defect (`L5586–5595`):** `analyze_market` instantiates a fresh `l3 = Level3OrderBookAnalyzer(...)` (`self._ref_mid = 0.0`) on **every cycle** (`L5586`), runs `for ev in _fresh_l3_order_events(...): l3.process_order_event(ev)` at **`L5589–5591`**, and only sets `self._ref_mid = px_f` inside `_feed_direct_trade_ticks` at **`L5594` (AFTER all MBO events were already processed!)**. Inside `_near_weight(price)` (`L1759`), `if price <= 0 or self._ref_mid <= 0: return 1.0` therefore returned **`1.0` on 100% of MBO events at every distance in the entire order book on every cycle**, rendering `L3_NEAR_TOUCH_USD = 2.50` (`U-06`) completely inert (`0/5` days, `-794.6` net)! **Fix:** Initialize `l3._ref_mid = mid_or_price` **before** `L5589` (`process_order_event`) and return `0.0` when `_ref_mid <= 0`.
   - **Bug #2 — `iceberg` `$12` Stale Radius & Liquidity-Magnet Defect (`L3416–3480`):** `_ib_max_dist = max(2.5*atr, price*0.003)` (`~$12` on `$4,000` Gold!) picks the highest-score entry in `iceberg_meta` regardless of whether `last_seen` was `45` minutes ago or price already smashed through the level, then blindly votes `best_price < price -> BUY` / `>= price -> SELL` (`0/5` days, `40.0%` right, `-2.061/c`). Per **Frey & Sandås (2009 / *QJF* 2017)**, active icebergs act as **liquidity magnets** attracting opposite market sweeps until exhausted or defended. **Fix:** Restrict `iceberg` to **recent (`now - last_seen <= 180s`), tight (`<= max(1.0*ATR, $1.50)`), side-verified, unbroken** icebergs (`bid` iceberg held `<= price` with upward bounce; `ask` iceberg held `>= price` with downward bounce).
   - **Bug #3 — `volume_roc` 1-Bar Noise & Climax-Wick Defect (`L2487, L3724`):** Computes `(vol[-1] - vol[-2]) / vol[-2] > 0.35` against **1 single prior bar** and votes `closes[-1] > closes[-2] -> BUY` (`0/5` days, `-501.7` net), firing after every sleepy 1-minute lull and buying blow-off shooting-star climax wicks! **Fix (*López de Prado 2018*; VSA):** Compute `RVOL` vs trailing `20`-bar median volume (`>= 1.8x`) and require candle body displacement (`|C - O| >= 0.45 * (H - L)` and `CLV >= 0.65` for `BUY` / `<= 0.35` for `SELL`).

#### D. Complete Forensic Dossier of All Retired (`14`), Dormant (`5`) & Previous (`4`) Bench Judges (`2026-10-31`)
*Owner Order (`2026-10-31`, Pre-Stage-2 Fair Trial):* Audit every retired (`config.RETIRED_JUDGES`), dormant (`_ghost_on`), and previous (`v5.8.1` / `U-16`) judge and all helper functions line-by-line against primary microstructure books, fix any grading/code bugs that tainted historical verdicts, and score all `25` bench & repaired variants side-by-side in `tools/raw_replay.py` Section **`[7b]`** (`100%` non-voting shadow) before Stage 2 panel surgery.

**Four Smoking-Gun Code & Parser Bugs Uncovered During the Bench Audit:**
1. **`vwap_zscore` (`step2_market_analysis.py:3651–3657` vs `judge_panel.py:131–145` & `step2_market_analysis.py:4245–4257`) — GRADED 100% BACKWARDS IN EVERY HISTORICAL AUDIT!**
   - `SignalEngine` (`L3655`) already gates `100%` to `regime == "RANGE"` & `abs(z) > 1.5`, scales adaptive weight (`0.8` when `vol_rank < 0.5` else `0.4`), and votes **`-np.sign(z)`** (`SELL -1` when `z > 0`, `BUY +1` when `z < 0`), emitting `"VWAP z-score 2.1 -> mean reversion fade (adaptive 0.40)"`.
   - Both copies of `parse_judge_panel` (`judge_panel.py:144` & `step2_market_analysis.py:4256`) fell into `elif lead: d = 1.0 if lead > 0 else -1.0`, grading `+2.1` as `BUY (+1.0)` and `-2.1` as `SELL (-1.0)` — **inverting 100% of `vwap_zscore` calls in past scoreboards!** Fixed in both `parse_judge_panel` copies (`2026-10-31`) and evaluated cleanly in `[7b]`.
2. **`supply_demand` (`L3748–3788`, `_detect_order_blocks` `L4503–4535`, `_nearest_zones` `L4538–4543`) — RETIRED BEFORE `U-14` KEY FIX WAS REPLAYED + UNMITIGATED ZONE DEFECT:**
   - Retired in `43e8d33` based on pre-`U-14` logs where `_nearest_zones` looked up `z["low"]`/`z["high"]` while `_detect_order_blocks` wrote `"bottom"`/`"top"` (`nearest_support/resistance` was broken!).
   - Furthermore, `_detect_order_blocks` (`L4516–4535`) builds noisy `M1` `k=3` (`3m`) pivots **without pruning mitigated zones** (zones smashed through 30 minutes ago remain active as "support/resistance"!), without checking departure impulse (`>= 1.0 * ATR`), and without a `TREND` seatbelt. Section `[7b]` tests both `supply_demand` (legacy) and `supply_demand[fix]` (`M5` **unmitigated** zones + `>= 1.0 ATR` departure impulse + `TREND` seatbelt).
3. **`htf_poc` (`L3789–3828`, `L5676–5686`) — INVERTED MAGNET SIGN AT `<= 1.0 ATR`:**
   - `analyze_market` (`L5676–5686`) builds valid trailing `H1` (`60` M1) and `H4` (`240` M1) volume POCs, **but `L3797–3804` (`abs(price - h1_poc) <= 1.0 * ATR`) votes `+1 BUY` when `price > h1_poc` and `-1 SELL` when `price < h1_poc`** — pushing *away* from the magnet and whipsawing on every 1-tick cross of fair value (*Steidlmayer 1986*; *Dalton 2013* equilibrium)! Meanwhile `> 2.0 ATR` (`L3808–3815`) fades without `regime == "RANGE"`. Section `[7b]` tests `htf_poc` vs `htf_poc[fix]` (silent inside `<= 0.6 ATR` equilibrium; `RANGE` `>= 2.0 ATR` fade *toward* `H1 POC`; `TREND` `>= 0.8 ATR` `H1+H4` value-migration alignment).
4. **`whale_walls` (`L3316–3381`) — `"BID" in "BID_CANCEL"` BUG + `$40` (`1%`) RADIUS:**
   - `L3338` scans `order_events[-500:]` with `if "BID" in ev_type: whale_bids.append(...)`, matching **`"BID_CANCEL"`** — counting **canceled/pulled spoofed whale bids as bullish support** across a `$40` (`1%`) band! Section `[7b]` tests `whale_walls` vs `whale_walls[fix]` (**live resting `order_book` only**, excluding `spoof_levels`, `<= max(2.0*ATR, $1.50)`, `TREND` seatbelt).

**Complete 25-Entry Bench Roster Audited & Wired into `tools/raw_replay.py` `[7b]`:**

### 💪 5. POWER v2 (the strength judge; configured clock)
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the strong man. He watches 5 TV channels about the market, and only speaks when the wind is REALLY blowing one way — and he says WHY every time.

*The lineage (why he exists — verified 10-17 from the retired design docs in git history):* **Age 1** (Sept): every judge voted a direction and the robot averaged them — the old panel scored 40.9 / 47.8 / 41.7% right at 15/30/60 min; a coin does better. **Age 2** (POWER v1, "the legs"): jobs not votes — only a few judges may pick a side, the rest amplify/weaken/cancel. **Age 3** (POWER v2, built 10-01, ACTIVE): the force specialist on closed bars at the active configured interval (M5 by source default; other intervals only when configured) — family caps so correlated readers share one budget, hard gates, NEITHER as an honest answer, reason codes, a memory book; v1 fully retired (zero calls in the live tree — "never fall back to a legacy direction"). The classroom was never deleted: it measures belief; POWER supplies directional force, subject to the configured execution gates.

**📦 Inside (9 parts):** ① **Bar builder** (closed bars only, from trade prints — POWER computes its OWN readings from raw prints on the `POWER_BAR_SECONDS` clock, derived from `TIMEFRAME` by default; the source default is M5/300s, and explicit configuration can select M3/180s; it never reads the classroom's votes) ② **Regime ladder** (ADX(14) on 28 active-timeframe bars: ≥25 TREND / <20 RANGE / between UNKNOWN; in RANGE it stays NEITHER — breakout authorization deliberately OFF) ③ **5 force channels in 3 families** (executed_flow 60%: footprint_delta + l3_aggr_limit + cvd_momentum; large prints 20%: big_prints — a POWER-only instrument the classroom doesn't have; breadth 20%: footprint_levels; the roster wall is enforced in code — any other judge name is turned away) ④ **Gates**: activity ≥0.18, dominance ≥0.60, ≥3 valid channels, ≥2 of 3 families agreeing ⑤ **Force-share calculator** (up/down %, sums to 100 — shares, never probabilities) ⑥ **Memory book** ⑦ **Voice gain 3.0** ⑧ **Reason-code engine** (every verdict says why) ⑨ **Warm-up controller + Local Active-TF Bar Cache (`2026-10-28`)** (28 bars at the active interval; untagged M5 probe rows remain rejected for non-M5 runs, while verified completed live `M3` OHLC bars persist atomically to `data/power_v2_memory/regime_bars_<bar_seconds>s.json` with symbol + price-scale checks so mid-day restarts recover `28/28` immediately instead of waiting `84` minutes).

*Known gap (honest, reported in every decision):* `l3_aggr_limit` is NOT EMITTED by the M5 adapter (no validated L3 executed-flow field mapped) — the executed_flow family runs at 65% coverage until it is wired.

*Now (10-16):* 93% NEITHER on the flat lake — correct discipline. *Problems:* 0.18 activity constant UNVALIDATED (patient #2); warm-up after restarts eats fish (17:06 on 10-16). *Improve:* activity-bucket grading.

*Repo audit (2026-10-21; source-only, not an owner-machine receipt):* `TIMEFRAME=M3` + `A1_BASE_TF_MINUTES=3` correctly produce `POWER_BAR_SECONDS=180`. But `classify_m5_regime()` calls `last_completed_m5_end()` without passing the configured `BAR_SECONDS`, so its boundary remains the default 300s while it buckets 180s bars. Synthetic M3 tape at 12:07:30Z returned `bar_end=12:05:00Z`, `NO_TRADE_IN_LATEST_COMPLETED_M5_BAR` (correct M3 boundary 12:06:00Z); an aligned sample at 12:15:05Z returned `ADX_TREND`. The M3 regime can therefore be unavailable between common boundaries; existing tests do not lock the off-boundary case. This mismatch arose when Fusion v1 switched the POWER clock to TIMEFRAME-driven M3 on 2026-10-17 (`ee284b8`) without passing the configured interval to the old M5 boundary helper; it remains in the latest feature branch. The probe writer/loader are fixed to M5/300s: `LOADED 28` proves 28 M5 rows parsed, not 28 contiguous M3 bars. At M3 the classifier rejects incompatible/gapped probe bars and must still warm from live M3 bars (fail-closed).

*Resolution (source-only, 2026-10-21):* the local replacement passes active `BAR_SECONDS` into completion-boundary, bucketing, labels and gap checks. Synthetic off-boundary M3 and missing-bar tests assert 12:06Z completion and fail-closed gap behavior. The M5 archive replay now pins 300s for its work and restores the caller's interval afterward, with a cross-caller regression test. Step 2 refuses fixed-M5 probe rows at non-M5 intervals and records probe/regime counts separately. Tests passed; no app/feed/account was run at that source-only check. A later owner-pasted 2026-10-22 11:44–11:50 Budapest loop shows M3 `regime_tail=28/28`, `gaps=0`, POWER NEITHER/RANGE, while the fixed-M5 probe has `loaded=0`, `usable=0`, and is incompatible. This is consistent with the active-M3 warm-up split, but does not test the 30–34 shadow-only candidate path.

### 🎯 6. Shooting (the planner — draws plans, never sends)
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the architect. Before any trip: checks the road is fair, draws the exact plan — which door, where the safety net is, whether the prize is worth the risk. He NEVER presses the go-button; he only hands the plan to the grown-ups.

**📦 Inside (8 parts):** ① **Quote sanity** (uncrossed bid/ask, spread agrees) ② **Door selector** (nearest viable door on POWER's side) ③ **CROWD v2** — the Three Fair Questions (its own section below) ④ **Geometry engine** (target = door − buffer; stop = book shelter or 2×ATR fallback; R:R math). *Wall laws: a shelter counts only if it passes the spoof trust filter — a fake wall is a trap, not a friend; a front wall too close = "no room to run" = skip; the buffer ≥ 1 spread, ideally 2 (spread eats scalps)* ⑤ **Cost check** (reward >3× spread; RR ≥1.2; USD floor) ⑥ **Queue validator** (with queue guard enabled, known-low fill probability <0.70 blocks passive; unknown/stale is not an unconditional veto; age ≤5s and quote-aligned when present) ⑦ **Style chooser** (patient limit by default; market ONLY through the A2 trigger) ⑧ **Plan writer** (30-second expiry, full receipts on every answer).

*Now (10-16):* rig 28/28 + 168/168 after v2; every pass AND veto prints receipts. *Improve:* v2.1 exact queue-ahead from our own mbo.csv.

This was introduced on 10-20 and cannot explain earlier no-trade days. Companion-report caveat: “queue ≥0.70” is stricter than the source logic; a known-low estimate blocks a passive route, while unknown is not itself a veto when passive policy is enabled.

*Source correction (2026-10-21; pushed to review branch 2026-10-22):* commit `70ba1bc20fd0df2b6be9aff925f2f4ebc5b766cc` on `shadow-only-review`; not merged to default. Owner-pasted runtime now shows matching M3/receipt/threshold/A2/live-flag signatures, but the process SHA is absent. `plan_ttl` is assigned before every receipt path; WAIT and GO plans carry structured receipts, specific sub-checks, model/source and execution context. Disabled passive policy is not converted to GO: eligible limits are `SHADOW_ONLY`, `execution_enabled=false`, and paper simulation receives no executable plan. Decision logging separates actual AI model from `decision_source`, safely migrates the verified legacy header, and refuses unknown extra-width rows rather than rewriting them. **📦 Inside (3 questions + 1 memory):** ① **Q1 SERVED** — door ≥ 3× our own shot (shot = trade_lots, fallback LOT_SIZE). ② **Q2 NORMAL ROAD** — road ≤ 2× its own rolling-median history (45-min window; fail-closed until 12 samples; `34cbc8a` `2026-10-28`: `_observe_passive_road_sample` warms `12/12` on fresh non-`GO` cycles in `12m` so `Q2` never starves). ③ **Q3 SMALL FOOTPRINT** — shot ≤ 10% of recent traded volume (last 500 prints). ④ **The road-history memory** (the guard's notebook of recent roads).

### 🧩 10. The Support Organs (L3, News, Volatility, Warm-up)
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the helpers behind the scenes: the order-book counter, the news clock, the ruler that measures how jumpy the market is, and the teacher who says "wait until you've seen enough."

### 📜 The Company Constitution (the Agreement Book, signed 2026-09-25 — absorbed here 10-17)

**The nine laws all teams obey — not suggestions:**
1. **One job, never two.** The scout never picks direction; the legs never draw the map.
2. **Say something the tape can check.** "Looks bullish" is not allowed; "touches 4400 in 20 minutes" is.
3. **Measure before you build.** No rule enters because it sounds clever — only if it survived the tape.
4. **No silent ceilings.** Anything capped, skipped or refused must say so out loud (the 24.5% squash lesson).
5. **Missing data is named, never hidden.** An unreadable day never touches an average.
6. **"Nothing" is always a legal answer.** A team forced to have an opinion will invent one — and it looks real in the log.
7. **Nobody trades until their homework is marked.** New teams write the diary first; they touch decisions only after real days graded them (the shadow-first law).
8. **Fail as one line, never as a dead loop.** A broken organ writes one note; the machine carries on.
9. **Small and reversible.** One change at a time, each with an off switch.

**The four charters:** **Scout** — knows where the doors are, minute by minute; NEVER says which way price will go. **POWER** — says which door price touches first, and how sure; NEVER averages judges that answer different questions. **Shooting** — decides if the shot is worth it and where target and stop go; NEVER shoots because the door is big — big doors with crowded roads are traps (the ancestor of Crowd v2). **Escort** — keeps an open trade safe; NEVER widens a stop, NEVER adds size (the ONE LAW).

**How they fit (the original dialog):** Scout: "a 23-lot door at 4400, five minutes, growing" ← FACTS · POWER: "push down hard, no divergence → floor first, 0.55" ← JUDGEMENT · Shooter: "corridor crowded but push strong, shelter 0.9 above — GO SELL, target 4400.60" ← DECISION · Escort: protects it ← SAFETY.

**The closing rule:** anything that breaks a MUST NOT is rejected, however well it performs — a rule broken once for a good reason gets broken forever for bad ones.

### Factory words (the vocabulary)

**Door** — a pile of resting orders (a target). **Road** — what stands between us and the door. **Shelter** — a wall behind us to lean the stop on. **Force shares** — POWER's up/down % (evidence, never probability). **Reason code** — every POWER verdict's "because". **Confidence** — the panel's agreement % (0–100). **Strength** — how loud the panel shouted (the AI bell needs ≥6). **Warm-up** — the honest waiting time (28 bars). **Fail-closed** — when unsure, say NO. **Receipt** — the numbers printed with every decision, so the evening can grade it. **Card** — a judge's graded history (weather = 1 day, climate = 5+). **Weather vs climate** — one day proves nothing; the 5-day cards decide.

## COMPANY 2 — THE REPORT OFFICE (after-hours truth-tellers)

### 📋 1. The Evening Paper (`bash report_evening.sh DATE`)
🧸 **For a 5-year-old:** the school report card of the whole day. Eight teachers each grade the robot from their own angle — and three separate calculators must tell the same story, or we don't trust the report.

**📦 Inside (8 desks + 1 law):**
1. **The Accountant** — 🧸 reads the diary and tells what every stopped trade WOULD have done. *Technical: decision ledger; counterfactual pts per stopping gate.*
2. **The Geometer** — 🧸 tries different prize distances on the same trades. *Technical: exits replayed at M3/M5/M8/M15 geometries (per-TF ATR).*
3. **The Shadow** — 🧸 the robot grades its own walls. *Technical: robot's own walls/brackets/trail vs fixed ruler on the same signals.*
4. **The Lens** — 🧸 glasses that see time faster or slower. *Technical: trend brain at M2–M10; unique moments, fish windows.*
5. **The Anatomist** — 🧸 the fish cutter: how big, how deep the water, who saw it. *Technical: travel ladders, heat before payoff, panel crossmatch ±5 min.*
6. **The Universes** — 🧸 five robot brothers with different clocks. *Technical: the REAL brain replayed at M1/M3/M5/M8/M10, same ruler. Fixed in `37df464` (`.env` TF isolation + offline memory/A2 protection; verified on `2026-10-23`: distinct bells `703/0`, `235/468`, `142/561`, `89/614`, `72/631`, `0` errors).*
7. **The Clocks** — 🧸 the patience teacher (see the Clocks Machine below). *Technical: C1/C2/C3/C6 verdicts.*
8. **The AI Cost line** — 🧸 how much the smart friend cost today. *Technical: paid calls, throttled rests, bells lost.*
+ **The Reconciliation Law** — 🧸 three calculators must agree within 2 points. *Technical: Accountant vs Geometer vs Shadow benchmark; 10-16 and 10-23 reconciled at `0.00`.*

```bash
bash report_evening.sh              # auto-picks newest trading log day; runs ALL tests + ALL Company 2 desks; opens data/report_<DATE>.html
bash report_evening.sh 2026-10-23   # same, for a specific trading date
bash report_evening.sh --fast       # ~45s quick evening run (skips slow replay Desks 4-6 & flow_probe)
bash report_evening.sh --open       # re-open the latest master HTML report immediately without re-running
```

**What this single command runs in dependency order (6 stages):**
1. **Toolkit & Code Self-Tests:** `daily_check.sh --check` (fail-closed auditor self-test), `python -m pytest -q`, `rig_g11.py`, `rig_memory.py`, and `rig_tf_robot.py` (skipped in `--fast` or `--no-tests`), written to `data/evening_test_gate_<DATE>.txt/.json`.
2. **Setup Preflight + 17-Test Day Audit + Multi-Day History:** `preflight.sh`, `audit_day.py` (all 17 tests, 33-judge scoreboard, AI veto record, 4 embedded diagnostics `real_roster`/`signal_health`/`depth_profile`/`force_study`, 5-day cross-day view, decisions/trades/judges drill-downs), and `tools/check_history.py --html` — run *before* Desk 7 so `clocks_verdict.py` always includes the freshly audited day.
3. **The Truck Camera & Signal Doctors:** `tail_probe.py --date <DATE> --peek`, `tools/signal_health.py`, and `tools/signal_map.py`.
4. **The Flow & Book Spies:** `flow_probe.py --date <DATE>` and `book_probe.py --date <DATE>` (now ON by default in full runs; `--no-book-probe` or `--fast` skips).
5. **Today's Paper & Reconciliation Law:** Desks 1 (`decision_ledger`), 2 (`tf_study`), 3 (`shadow_replay`), 9 (`power_report_card`), 10 (`council_shadow`), 4 (`tf_lens`), 5 (`fish_anatomy`), 6 (`tf_robot_replay`), 7 (`clocks_verdict`), Watchman, Desk 8 (`the AI COST line`), and 3-desk `RECONCILIATION` (`data/evening_paper_<DATE>.txt`).
6. **Desk 11 (`The X-Ray`) & Unified Browser Report (`dc27148` + `7c35678`):** `tools/xray_report.py` writes **`data/xray_<DATE>.txt`** (one secret-redacted, no-`.zip` shareable `.txt` containing `[X1]–[X9]` Step 1→5 funnel, log/PnL digest, and embedded Artifact Vault) and `tools/report_office_bundle.py` embeds Stages 1–6 (`#xray_overview` + 1-click Copy buttons) into **`data/report_<DATE>.html`** + **`data/evening_suite_<DATE>.log`**.

### 📔 THE REPORT OFFICE DICTIONARY — know them better and wiser

| Desk | 5-word job | Health | Best receipt |
|---|---|---|---|
| Evening Paper | eight teachers, one truth | 🟢 | reconciled `0.00` (`10-16..10-28`) |
| Day Audit | the doctor's 17 checks | 🟢 | `12P/5W/0F` on `10-28` (`df5e82a`) |
| Truck Camera | films the slow parts | 🟢 | `STEP3` median `10ms` (`55` `-cached` slips on `10-28`) |
| Clocks Machine | the patience teacher | 🟢 | `C3` shadow walls `t = -3.29` (`1,156` trades) |
| Universe Machine | five brothers, five clocks | 🟢 | M1/M3/M5/M8/M10 distinct (`37df464`) |
| Probes & Doctors | flow, MBO & wall checks | 🟢 | `0.0%` orphan MBO cancels across `1.04M` orders (`10-28`) |
| **Desk 11: X-Ray** | **36 files -> 1 shareable `.txt`** | 🟢 | **`36/36 PRESENT` on `2026-10-28` (`dc27148` + `7c35678`)** |
| Watchman | the night guard | 🔴 (never ran) | — |
| Briefings | numbers → story | 🟢 | #1, #2, #3 + nightly X-Ray briefings |

**Report words:** **What-if** — if we HAD taken every signal, what would have happened. **Counterfactual pts** — the money a stopped trade would have made/lost. **Ruler** — the frozen scoring geometry (SL 2×ATR / TP 3.5×ATR / 180 min / 0.5 cost — the triple-barrier method). **Fish** — a moment with ≥1.00 pts of favourable travel after costs. **Heat** — how far price goes AGAINST you before paying. **Trendiness** — 0 = flat lake, 1 = one-way river. **Shadow** — the robot's own exits replayed. **Reconciliation** — three calculators, one story. **Rung 1/2** — evidence levels for any timeframe change (1 = informs, 2 = full re-vote + review). **Coverage** — what fraction of moments a gate lets through. **WR / PF** — win rate / profit factor. **Weather vs climate** — one day vs five-day cards.

## 📎 ANNEX TO CHAPTER 1 — THE TWO WITNESSES: THE COMPLETE CLASSROOM-vs-POWER COMPARISON
*(owner's order 10-17: keep every detail — this annex is the full record)*

### The team-structure truth (verified 10-17 from the retired docs in git history)

- **The OLD system (September):** ONE monolithic brain — the judges panel inside step2 did the whole process alone (direction, levels, everything), then handed the result to the AI gate. The one-room school.
- **The NEW system (designed 09-25):** the school's jobs were carved into specialist teams. First proposal (TEAMS_DESIGN.md): 3 teams — POWER (direction, before the trade) · SIGNAL (permission + where, at the trade) · ESCORT (safety, after the trade). The Agreement Book split SIGNAL into two charters → **the four teams: SCOUT (the map) · POWER (the force) · SHOOTING (the plan) · ESCORT (the protection)**. POWER was always ONE team of the new system, never the whole thing (owner's memory confirmed).
- **Where that left the classroom:** the four teams took direction, map, plan, and protection; the panel kept the one job nobody took — measuring AGREEMENT (belief + loudness). It lives on as door 4 + the AI's bell, demoted not deleted, graded nightly.
- **POWER's own journey:** the design doc's POWER was "14 judges with weights" (the panel reorganized); v1 ("the legs", jobs-not-votes); v2 (10-01) abandoned judges-as-votes for own-computed channels from raw prints — the three ages (see POWER's section).

### Identity

| | 🏫 Classroom (configured A1 clock; M3 was the 10-19 observation) | 💪 POWER v2 (configured clock; M5 source default) |
|---|---|---|
| Soul | "Do we agree something is happening — and how loudly?" | "Is there a REAL one-way push — and why?" |
| Kind | an opinion survey (belief meter) | a measurement (force meter) |
| Born | September (v5.9→v8, 33 judges) | 10-01 (v2; v1 mid-Sept) |
| Crime/fix | as direction caller: 40.9/47.8/41.7% @15/30/60m — a coin does better | took direction away from the vote |
| Job today | confidence/strength context for the configured Step-3/4 gates; not an independent order permission | POWER direction + force evidence; any configured-threshold carry must still meet the owner gate; below-threshold candidates are shadow-only |

### Machinery, detail by detail

| Detail | 🏫 Classroom | 💪 POWER |
|---|---|---|
| Input | 5 data worlds (tape, book, order events, structure, macro) | raw trade prints only |
| Clocks | configured A1/base timeframe (M3 in the dated 10-19 observation) | closed active-timeframe bars + 28-bar ADX; M5 probe is not relabeled at M3 |
| Members | ~33 judges, 5 families; ~21–25 speak daily | 5 channels, 3 families; 2–4 live typically |
| Families | Footprint(7) · Order book(7) · Hidden(7) · Structure(8) · Macro(5) | executed_flow 60% cap · large prints 20% · breadth 20% |
| Correlation control | none hard-coded (independence from different windows; measured: conf unmoved by POWER) | FAMILY CAPS BY LAW — correlated flow readers share one 60% budget |
| Votes | BUY/SELL/QUIET weighted 0.4–1.5 | signed −1…+1 with quality 0–1; missing = EXCLUDED never zero |
| Output | direction + confidence 0–100 + strength 0–100 | UP/DOWN/NEITHER + force shares + activity + coverage + reason code |
| Honest answer | mumbles when confused (median conf 22–25) | NEITHER proudly, with a reason (11-word vocabulary) |
| Memory | judges' cards (C1/C2 nightly) | hash-chained diary, one record per bar |
| Fail mode | fail-loud | fail-closed (bad data → NEITHER) |
| Downstream needs | AI prompt, confidence/strength context, reporting/C6; does not lower the configured Step-4 gate | Shooting direction + reason code; configured-threshold carry is separate from shadow candidates |

### The receipts scoreboard

| Contest | Classroom | POWER | Winner |
|---|---|---|---|
| Direction calling | 40.9/47.8/41.7% (the old crime) | ~59% @+30m, ~63% @+60m (22 calls, 2 days — weather) | POWER |
| Belief metering | C6 flips at 50 (twice); 40–49 = −981/10d; saved −483.6 (10-16); +965.6 refusals (10-15); A3: loud 83% → quiet 29% fish payoff | dominance dial SATURATED (all calls 80–100%, 2–4 live votes) — ungradeable until roster fills | Classroom |
| Flat-lake discipline | brave 13% of minutes — all would have lost (183 signals = −514.3) | 93% NEITHER, all correct | POWER |
| Only head-to-head (1 co-brave minute in 2 days) | disagreed, lost at +30m | disagreed, WON | POWER (sample: 1) |
| Self-improvement | C1/C2 cut the whale; found vwap_bands MVP (97%, +4.28) | card born 10-17 — 2 days of data | Classroom |
| Speaking wisdom | speaks most when confused | speaks only when real: 7% on the lake, 50% in the living evening | POWER |
| Starvation | 0–1 co-brave minutes/day (both days) | same number, shared disease | tie — the Council's reason |

### What each can NEVER see

**Classroom sees, POWER never will:** the map (doors/roads/shelters) · hidden players (icebergs, spoofs, sweeps) · macro weather (DXY, VIX, yields, risk) · structure (VWAP bands, POCs, value areas, supply/demand) · the news calendar's meaning.
**POWER sees, the classroom only feels vaguely:** true executed force on one clean clock · big prints' signed weight (money, not headcount) · force CHANGE (CVD momentum) · breadth (one price or many).

### Failure modes

**Classroom fails by:** confidently agreeing on something weak (the 40–49 trap) · correlated enthusiasm on one-theme days · starving the machine (belief bottleneck) · feeding the over-confluence trap.
**POWER fails by:** single-domain blindness (a real wind under a black macro sky) · saturation (2–4 votes can saturate dominance) · warm-up fragility (28 active bars; the dead-probe cost was 60 bars on 10-16; M3 now refuses fixed-M5 rows and warms from contiguous live M3 bars) · honest silence on thin markets (the 0.18 question) · the unbuilt L3 channel (heaviest family at 65%).

### The verdict

Champions of different contests — each already failed at the other's job. The classroom tried direction for weeks (41.7% at the hour, coin-grade) — disqualified; its belief record is the company's most proven number. POWER cannot yet be a belief meter (saturated dial, one world of five); as direction caller he is ~63% at the hour with the slow-bloom signature (29% @15m → 71% @30m → 71% @60m; median travel −0.90 → +1.10 → +3.70) and perfect lake discipline. The mathematics (Condorcet + ensemble diversity) and the receipts agree: a diverse jury and one deep specialist are complements — delete either and you lose a mechanism the other cannot replace. The strongest single fact we own: **the starvation number, 0–1 co-brave minutes a day** — two correct, independent, rarely-brave witnesses AND-ed together produce silence. That is the architecture asking for the Council (parked, docket).

🧸 *The friends with 34 windows are the best at shouting "we all agree!" only when true. The strong man is the best at knowing which way the wind really blows. The friends once steered the cart into a ditch, so the strong man took the wheel; the strong man has one window, so the friends became his eyes. The king keeps both, grades both nightly — and has already drawn the plans for the day they share one desk.*

## The research verdicts (receipts behind the designs)

**Crowd Math v2 (10-16, 24 sources).** The old road÷door ratio compares two static depth stocks — nobody professional does this. The causal pairs: flow-vs-depth (Cont–Kukanov–Stoikov: ΔP ≈ β·OFI, R² 65%, slope ∝ 1/depth); size-vs-volume (square-root law I ≈ Y·σ·√(Q/V); BofA participation tiers 5–30%); size-vs-door-depth (fill prob ≈ traded-through ÷ (queue-ahead + size)). Our order size was missing entirely. Displayed depth can lie (ESMA ghost 4–7%; Globex >25% canceled <2s) → rolling medians only. We own CME MBO → v2.1 exact queue-ahead. Key sources: arxiv.org/abs/1011.6402 · bouchaud.substack.com · BofA electronic trading guide · esma.europa.eu WP 4/2020 · cmegroup.com MBO FAQ · arxiv.org/abs/2403.02572.

**Gate audit (10-16, 20 sources).** Feed fail-closed = FIA best practice; risk door = SEC 15c3-5 + CFTC TAC + MiFID II Art. 17 (law); doorman = MAS SAFR 2026 shape (deterministic layer above the AI — most academic LLM traders lack it; we have it); conf gate = the MDPI threshold method (our C6 exactly); regime/confluence filters cut trade count without improving win rate ("−74% trades, same WR"). The disease has a name: the over-confluence trap. Key sources: sec.gov 34-63241 · fia.org WP 2024 · mas.gov.sg SAFR · arxiv 2609.19705 · halleytech.substack.com · mdpi.com 2076-3417/15/20/11145.

**A6 methodology verdict (10-14).** Our TF comparison = published best practice (triple-barrier, one frozen ruler, no look-ahead, costs charged). Five honesty rules: the winner of N candidates is flattering (False Strategy Theorem); 5 days is weather; signals cluster; what-if measures SIGNAL quality; the big ruler punishes fast clocks.

**A3 measured fish (10-12, 4-day census).** Payoff +1pt/9min: 83/75/60/29% loud→quiet; median heat 0.2–0.9 pts; ladder +1.4→+2.0 by 9–12 min (river monster +5.5→+16.8); **curve flattens after ~9 minutes** → burst exits: SL 1.8, TP +1.8/+4.0 river, 9-min time-stop, never widen.

**The external reviews (10-14, adjudicated).** Both wrong-branch, fabricated numbers, recommended live trading (rejected — demo-only law). Useful direction only: judge thresholds → whale cut, already decided on our own receipts.

## The laws we discovered (and live by)

1. **STRUCTURE FROM STANDARDS, NUMBERS FROM RECEIPTS.** 2. **The dimensional law** — never compare two raw stocks of different things. 3. **The over-confluence trap** — every extra AND-gate multiplies silence; the cure is per-filter grading. 4. **Displayed depth can lie** — rolling medians only. 5. **Old tests are tombstones** — after a deliberate policy change, reds are the old policy's witnesses. 6. **The anchor law** — patch only against true live files, verbatim, once-each (5 stale-snapshot incidents to date). 7. **Fail closed, print receipts.**

## The repair history in one line each

The trigger was blind → eyes (A2) · the panel was slow → bar clock (F8) · the keys jammed → doorman parking (F15) · the judges had no memory → report cards (F14) · the whale lost money → retired (F16) · the models were stale → rotated (F17) · the feed died silently → GoldBridge revived + keep-BookMap-open law · the console crashed on σ → ASCII-fold · the ruler was homemade → Crowd v2. **The chain is repaired link by link, every link receipted. What remains is the market's word.**

## Market-quality guard implementation status (2026-10-22)

1. **Immediate operating safeguard — unchanged:** `ALLOW_LIVE_TRADING=0` does not disable demo orders when `TRADING_ENABLED=True`. If the loop is being used only to inspect L2, pause the loop or disable demo order routing through the owner's normal process. No `.env` changes were made.
2. **P0 — L2/quote integrity and freshness — implemented in the clean copy:** per-level depth validation, contract/expiry match, crossed/locked-book suppression, quote/spread consistency, last-trade alignment, timestamp ages, and raw top-five diagnostics. BookMap entry preflight requires validated L2, matched instrument, and matching diagnostic quote. **Important provenance limit:** the bundled BookMap add-on stamps rows with local callback/bridge receive time; the recorded ages detect silence since receipt, but do not prove upstream exchange-event age or end-to-end vendor latency. The receipt labels this basis explicitly. No live feed was used to verify it.
3. **P1 — data-spread telemetry and fail-safe gate — implemented in the clean copy:** missing/invalid spread is `N/A`/blocked for live sources rather than a zero that skips the cap; quote and spread must agree; stale, future, crossed, unknown, and over-limit cases fail closed. Existing `MAX_SPREAD_PCT` and authorization settings are unchanged.
4. **P1 — independent spread protections — source/mock verified only:** tests exercise the data-feed guard and actual MT5 trade-symbol broker-tick guard independently; a mocked blocked broker spread produces no `order_send`. There is no runtime Step-4 order attempt in this receipt.
5. **P2 — latency and freshness — implemented in the clean copy:** feed/quote/depth/trade/order-event ages are recorded; monotonic elapsed pipeline time is added to captured feed/quote/depth age and the data-side check is repeated immediately before Step 4. The MT5 broker tick is checked again inside order placement. These are source/test results, not proof about the owner's running process.
6. **P2 — explicit L3 capability — implemented in the clean copy:** fresh timestamped events with recognized sides only; unknown aggressor sides are not fabricated; inferred candle/tick-rule sides are not called direct prints; capability labels distinguish market-order-only, passive-only, partial tags, unavailable, and synthetic demo evidence. Queue-ahead capability remains unavailable without a richer source.
7. **Shadow evidence — implemented in the clean copy:** timestamped market-quality evidence is attached after preflight to non-executable candidates and persisted after Step 4, without modifying eligibility or authorization. Keep the execution threshold at 35, retain existing caps/guards, and collect multi-day evidence before any separately approved gate change. “30” remains ambiguous: `POWER_LEAD_CONF_FLOOR=30` is shadow-only; `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD=30` changes the demo execution gate.

## 🏛️ THE COUNCIL DOSSIER — the fusion design study (10-17 overnight, owner-ordered)

*The question: how to use MORE of POWER — the new system's heart — without losing the classroom's proven independence. Worked alone through the night; everything below is design + evidence tooling. NOTHING installed — the week belongs to Crowd v2.*

### What the science says (researched 10-17)

- **Static combination loses to conditional gating.** Fixed weights and hard AND-gates are the weakest way to combine experts; a gate that decides *per situation* which expert to trust (mixture-of-experts, Jacobs 1991 → modern surveys) consistently wins — and gating on each expert's running confidence/performance is a proven gate design.
- **Every gate is a coverage–risk trade.** (Chow 1970; selective prediction.) Coverage = how often we act; selective risk = how wrong the acted moments are. Our machine sits at ~0.2% coverage with near-zero risk — the extreme corner. The honest question is never "open the doors" but **"how much coverage can we buy back per unit of risk"** — answered only by sweeping thresholds on recorded days.
- **A gate earns its keep only if the risk it removes is large relative to the coverage it consumes — and thresholds calibrated on one evidence regime (lake days) lie on another (river days).** So any new floor must be graded per regime, on multiple days, before it goes live.

### The four options

| Opt | Rule | What it is | Needs before live |
|---|---|---|---|
| **A — The Council** | floors (conf ≥30, dom ≥55, agree) + conviction = 0.5·conf + 0.5·dom ≥45 | compensatory fusion, one conviction number, both cards preserved | dominance calibration (L3+CVD must fill his roster) + 5-day shadow |
| **B — POWER-led floor** ⭐ | POWER speaks + agree + class floor drops 45→30 (or 20) | the specialist leads, the jury sanity-checks — conditional gating, the literature's answer | the matrix cell "conf 30-49 + POWER spoke" must grade POSITIVE on 3+ real days |
| C — sequential cascade | POWER speaks first; only then is the class consulted (saves AI calls too) | reordering, not relaxing | same evidence as B |
| D — POWER-only | POWER speaks, no class gate | full replacement | **REJECTED for now** — single-domain blindness + saturated dial; kept as the control arm |

**Recommendation: B is the bridge, A is the palace.** B uses the most POWER the soonest (the owner's wish) while keeping the jury's 5-world veto; A follows once the dominance dial calibrates.

### The decision instrument (built + rigged tonight)

**`council_shadow.py` + `rig_council_shadow.py` — RIG PASS (16 hand-verified assertions).** Read-only; runs on any recorded day from the diary + decisions log:

1. **THE MATRIX** — class-conf buckets × POWER state → outcomes. The critical cell: is the class's toxic 40–49 band still toxic *when real force confirms*?
2. **THE SWEEP** — Chow's curve: floors 45→20 → passed minutes + right% + median travel (coverage vs risk, one line per floor).
3. **DESIGNS** — TODAY vs OPT B (floor 30 / floor 20) vs Council v0 vs POWER-only, side by side.
4. **STRENGTH LADDER** — class loudness vs outcome when POWER spoke (A3's 83/29 gradient, now measurable per day).

### The first evidence (owner ran the mirror, 10-15 + 10-16)

- **TODAY passed ZERO minutes both days** (floor-45 sweep line: `passed 0`) — fusion-level starvation, measured.
- **The classroom earned its seat:** on 10-15, POWER alone (D) = 71 minutes, 55% right; POWER + class floor 30 + agreement (B) = 9 minutes, **100%** right (+6.20 median). The jury's check converts the specialist's 55% into 100% selectivity — the receipt that ends the delete-the-classroom question forever.
- **The matrix pattern:** when POWER spoke, every conf band grades well (70/71/100%); when POWER was silent, the class's confidence is anti-informative on the lake (conf ≥50 silent: 30% right, −7.50 median). Each witness fixes exactly the other's blindness.
- **Option B ledger (floor 30 + agree):** 16 passed minutes, 14 right (88%), positive medians both days; the critical cell (conf 40-49 + POWER spoke) = 100% on both days. Caveats: minutes not trades; small N; weather; 196 frozen-window minutes on 10-16 (manual closure) may pollute some marks.
- **Protocol status: 2 of 3+ evidence days.** Nightly runs continue; if the pattern holds → Option B design spec → owner review → 5-day shadow → one switch.

### The L3 channel (POWER's missing vote)

`l3_flow_probe.py` already sits in the repo — purpose-built to read the real mbo.csv and report whether the stream carries aggressor-side + executed-size fields. If yes → a small adapter patch (design review first) fills his heaviest family from 65% coverage. Evidence first, always. **VERDICT (owner ran it 10-17): the stream carries NO aggressor/side field (time, event_type, order_id, price, size, instrument only) → executed flow cannot be computed honestly → the silence is formally accepted; the 5th vote stays empty until a richer Bookmap export someday. The family-cap math already handles the gap via coverage.**

### The protocol (the laws, unchanged)

1. Crowd v2 owns this week — no fusion change before Briefing #3 + its card.
2. Owner runs `council_shadow.py` on 10-15 + 10-16 (and nightly from Monday) → the matrix and sweep fill with real days.
3. If the 30–49 + POWER cells grade positive on 3+ days → design review of Option B (owner reads, owner approves) → shadow beside the live gate 5 days → ONE switch, that week's one change.
4. Option A follows when the dials calibrate. Deletion of either witness: never.

## Source re-check — daily audit findings — 2026-10-22

**Read-only scope:** inspected the current sandbox copy's `power_v2_regime.py`, `step2_market_analysis.py`, and `audit_day.py`, including M3 regime construction, startup probe handling, the what-if replay, confidence baseline, and warm-up report parser. The owner's Windows checkout and generated report files are not directly mounted here; no app run, code edit, `.env` edit, or test run was performed for this review.

- **M3 warm-up:** real runtime state in the owner's late 2026-10-22 sample was `regime_tail=4/28`, latest contiguous run 4, one gap, regime UNKNOWN. Source requires 28 consecutive completed active-timeframe bars (`period=14`, 2×period); at M3, 28 bars span at least 84 minutes, and a gap invalidates readiness. The fixed-M5 probe is intentionally refused for M3; do not relabel M5 bars or lower the 28-bar rule. A genuine M3 backfill/continuous timestamped-print path is the runtime fix. The auditor is also M5-shaped: its regex expects `status=\w+` and `loaded_bars=`, while the M3 log uses a colon-bearing mismatch status and `loaded_probe_bars`/`regime_tail`; Test 10 equates probe `LOADED` with warm-up readiness. Thus the audit's probe/warm-up grades are not reliable at M3, although the direct 4/28 runtime line does prove readiness was incomplete at that sampled time.
- **422 skipped decisions:** do not attribute all skips to M3 warm-up. The supplied reason breakdown includes confidence-zero/below-threshold, HOLD, Shooting/POWER, AI/POWER disagreement, and operator no-new-entry blocks. The exact per-row cause remains the `decisions_log` receipt to inspect; the post-install 4/28 sample is later than most of the day's logged decisions.
- **What-if:** confirmed `audit_day.py:784–819, 1652+` builds five-minute candles and walks those with a 36-candle/180-minute default and M5 ATR assumptions. The −960.1-point figure is therefore an M5 hypothetical replay, not an M3-aligned strategy result or actual P&L. A report-only fix should select the day's recorded timeframe, build M3 candles when appropriate, preserve the same minutes-based holding horizon (60 M3 bars for 180 minutes), use timeframe-correct ATR/cost assumptions, and label any unavailable assumptions rather than silently substituting M5.
- **Confidence baseline:** `audit_day.py:3107` hard-codes expected `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD=50`, while the supplied run-config and owner-reported `.env` are 35. This is a stale/incorrect audit baseline for the reported day, not evidence the app ran at 50 or `.env` is wrong. Use a dated, owner-approved baseline (35 for the day if that is the intended setting) or separate “recorded run setting” from an explicit policy baseline; do not change `.env` to 50, and do not overwrite historical baselines globally.

**Disposition at that time:** the `70ba1bc` copy had two confirmed audit/report defects (M5 what-if and stale confidence baseline), one genuine M3 warm-up-not-ready sample plus an M3-unaware audit parser, and no evidence that warm-up alone caused all 422 skips. No code was changed in that 10-22 source re-check; preserve as historical evidence.

## Check-in stamp — BookMap replay and DEMO-test preparation — 2026-10-23

**🧸 The short story:** the robot was reading millions of old pages to find today's prices, then its tail-reader could read some of the newest pages again. The follow-up tells it where the first reading stopped, so it continues after that point. It now times each file. It still reads old pages once to decide they are stale, so this is a measured partial fix—not proof that the whole delay is gone.

### Current code and evidence

- **Base and branch:** owner reports Windows checkout `shadow-only-review` at `4f71043f27b64ba12c981a2c1285a7bcd01d20c8`. The latency/replay and DEMO-smoke follow-up was applied on top locally; it is not committed or pushed. Default `main` remains `3b8dc9d`; no merge occurred.
- **Owner's one-cycle receipt (11:49 Budapest, 2026-10-23):** command `python main.py --no-new-entries`. Replay source metrics are recorded in Chapter 1 and Chapter 2. Quote age was 45ms and L2 validated at acquisition; pre-execution data age reached 18.6s and later about 25s. `source_event_time_verified=false`, so the BookMap bridge receipt time cannot prove upstream/exchange latency. The Step-2 snapshot reported an L2 continuity reset after a source/instrument transition.
- **Decision/order:** the sample showed SELL strength 45.77, confidence 31.24; POWER M3 NEITHER and 11/28 warm-up bars (17 more needed); Shooting WAIT and Gemini HOLD. Step 4 was skipped by the explicit no-new-entry flag, Step 5 found no open trade, and `sent_to_mt5=0`. No order or fill occurred. This says nothing about what a future normal-loop cycle will decide.
- **Tests:** owner reports default and `TIMEFRAME=M3` suites each **314 passed + 3 subtests**. One default quality test failed once because the test fixture's fresh-depth check returned stale/missing; it passed alone on rerun and then the full default suite passed. Do not weaken live freshness checks. The patch also passed `git apply --check`; no `.env` change was made.

### Four-Pass Audit receipt — follow-up patch

1. **Read the actual changes:** reviewed `bookmap_bridge_provider.py`, `main.py`, `step4_mt5_execution.py`, `tools/demo_order_smoke.py`, `tools/DEMO_ORDER_SMOKE.md`, and the two new regression-test files. The provider change stores the live text-file seek cookie after history replay, logs scan counts/times/order regressions, and resumes catch-up from that cookie; fallback remains if the offset cannot be returned.
2. **Caller census:** `_replay_recorded_history()` is reached through `_catch_up()`; its new offset is consumed there. The unresolved-test marker is checked by `main._safety_gates()`, `main.run_step4()`, and independently by `MT5Executor.execute()` so direct strategy-executor calls also fail closed. The smoke tool is a separate operator command, not part of the regular strategy path.

Default and M3 suites passed as recorded above.
4. **Function-by-function safety receipt:** replay keeps stale rows in the scan and records ordering evidence without using it to skip data; the entry gates fail closed while the marker exists; the one-shot tool defaults to no order, demands explicit typed DEMO consent and an estimated-loss cap, rechecks broker/account/feed/risk state, verifies filled stops/risk, has no leave-open option, and keeps/requires recovery until all test-symbol positions and pending orders are gone. The sandbox had no MetaTrader5 package; no order, account, or feed was independently verified.

## Standing Nightly Audit, Patch & GitHub Upgrade Protocol (Windows UCRT64 Git Bash)

- **Owner Machine Constants (`A:\gitHub\Gold-BookMap` = `/a/gitHub/Gold-BookMap`):** Shell is **Windows MSYS2 UCRT64 Git Bash**; invoke pytest ONLY as **`python -m pytest -q`** (bare `pytest` is not on `PATH`; `python` = CPython `3.12.4`); browser downloads land in **`/c/Users/Jafar/Downloads/`** (`PATCH_FILE=$(ls -t /c/Users/Jafar/Downloads/<name>*.patch ./<name>*.patch 2>/dev/null | head -n 1)`).
- **Mandatory Pre-Delivery Test Laws for Windows Compatibility:** (1) Live `data/` and `logs/` on `A:\gitHub\Gold-BookMap` are **already populated** (`maintenance_state.json`, `pm_state.json`, `ai_calls.json`, `decisions_*.csv`, `logs/bot.log`) — state-isolation tests must assert **`before_fingerprint == after_fingerprint`**, never `not (live_data / ...).exists()` or `iterdir() == []`; (2) Windows `datetime.now(timezone.utc)` rounds `100ns` ticks half-to-even to integer microseconds (`+500ns` ahead of raw `time.time()` `~17.6%` of calls) — freshness checks must allow `-0.005s` (`5ms`) clock jitter while rejecting real future timestamps (`+1s`); (3) any inline Python helper writing repo files must pass **`newline="\n"`** (`p.write_text(..., encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")`) so Git never warns on `CRLF`; (4) owner `.env` sets **`V6_4TEAMS_ENABLED=false`** (classical weighted voting), **`AI_MAX_PROMPT_BARS=20`**, `CONFIDENCE_THRESHOLD=35`, `ALLOW_LIVE_TRADING=0`, `A2_BURST_MAX_PER_DAY=100`, and `RETIRED_JUDGES=...` — AND `analyze_market()` calls `config.reload_env()` (`load_dotenv(.env, override=True)`), which wipes `monkeypatch.setattr(config, ...)` back to `.env` values unless the test ALSO sets **`monkeypatch.setattr(config, "reload_env", lambda *a, **k: None)`** (always simulate a real `.env` file on disk in detached worktree verification before delivering any patch).
- **The `X-Ray` Full-Overview System (`bash report_evening.sh` -> 1 HTML + 1 Shareable `.txt`, NO `.zip` Needed):**
  - **20-File Core Roster + 11 Companion Artifacts (`data/`):** (1) `evening_suite_<DATE>.log`, (2) `evening_test_gate_<DATE>.txt` (+ `.json`), (3) `preflight_<DATE>.txt`, (4) `day_audit_<DATE>.txt`, (5) `day_metrics_<DATE>.json`, (6) `judge_panel_<DATE>.csv`, (7) `prices_<YYYYMMDD>.json`, (8) `history_summary_<DATE>.txt`, (9) `tail_probe_<DATE>.txt`, (10) `decision_ledger_<DATE>.txt` (+ `.csv`), (11) `shadow_replay_<DATE>.txt` (+ `.csv`), (12) `power_report_card_<DATE>.txt`, (13) `fish_anatomy_<DATE>.txt`, (14) `run_config_<YYYYMMDD>.json`, (15) `decisions_<YYYYMMDD>.csv`, (16) `report_<DATE>.html`, (17) `day_report_<DATE>.html`, (18) `decisions_<DATE>.html`, (19) `judges_<DATE>.html`, and (20) `scoreboard_<DATE>.html` — plus `trades_<DATE>.html`, `flow_probe_<DATE>.txt`, `book_probe_<DATE>.txt` (now ON by default in full runs), `signal_health_<DATE>.txt`, `signal_map_<DATE>.txt`, `council_shadow_<DATE>.txt`, `tf_study_<DATE>.txt`, `tf_lens_<DATE>.txt`, `tf_robot_<DATE>.txt`, `evening_paper_<DATE>.txt`, `clocks_verdict.txt`, and **`xray_<DATE>.txt` + `xray_<DATE>.json`**.
  - **Installed Desk 11 — `tools/xray_report.py` (`Gold-BookMap-xray-report-upgrade.patch`, `356 passed, 3 subtests`):** One `bash report_evening.sh` run executes `signal_health.py` + `signal_map.py` + `book_probe.py` + `xray_report.py` (including post-`tee` log refresh), embeds `#xray_overview` + 1-click Copy button into `data/report_<DATE>.html`, and writes **one universal plain-text file `data/xray_<DATE>.txt` (`~650 KB`, two-layer secret-redacted, zero `.zip` needed)** combining `[X1]–[X9]` (Step 1→5 funnel, directional Crowd/Shooting vetoes, Step 3 AI/async/key-pool routing, Step 4 COMEX-MT5 basis/spread/PosManager/Escort/TCA, Signal Health & Wall Map, cross-organ state-drift check, live trading-log error/warning digest, 17-test/10-desk scorecard, sanitized `run_config`, and 36-item manifest) with the embedded plain-text Artifact Vault (`--unpack` supported).
- **One-Shot Nightly Patch Apply & GitHub Push Template:** Stop `--loop` (`Ctrl+C`) -> `cd /a/gitHub/Gold-BookMap` -> verify `git rev-parse HEAD` matches patch base -> `git apply --check --whitespace=error "$PATCH_FILE" && git apply --whitespace=error "$PATCH_FILE" && python -m pytest -q && python tools/selftest_audit_day.py && git add <exact files> && git commit -m "..." && git push origin HEAD:shadow-only-review && git push origin HEAD:main` (chain with `&&` so commit/push halts automatically if any gate fails; **never** stage `.env`, `data/`, `logs/`, `Workspace/*.bmw`, `_attic_2026-10-17/`, `.jar`, backups, or `.patch`).

Fixed by scanning backward across the full `2*lookback+2` minute timestamp window and returning `0.0` (never stale `3h` `cvd`) when `<lookback` minutes exist. **Law #5 11-Day Check (`2026-10-16..30`):** `vwap_bands` (`-561.7` on Friday's `0.94` waterfall) is **`+249.6 pts` net positive across `11` days** (`+811.3 pts` across prior `10` days) -> **kept untouched per Law #5**; `cvd_divergence` was `-466.0 pts` across `11` days due to `B7` -> **code bug fixed, kept under multi-day watch**.
