Family Gate 165 EP; 3 proven-leak suspects GUILTY-HINT, all [THIN]: Non-Trend BUY +10.63/2 EP, Climax +4.80/3 EP, Opposes +1.36/3 EP). **PENDING:** 5-day tape `replay-41` completes the both-window check of `[8m]` (owner rig). THEN build `[8n]` gate mirror (lift/keep, both arms same day set, bar = beat GO control on 3d AND 5d, >=8 EP/window/touched-block). Book ceiling raised to < 4500.

**PHASE-1 BOTH-WINDOWS VERDICT (`2026-11-12`, `replay-41` 5d vs `replay-40` 3d — CLOSED: GATES STAY):** the wide window flips the suspect table. **Non-Trend BUY (3b Guard) ACQUITTED** — 3d +10.63/ep (2 EP) collapses to +0.13/ep (9 EP): window luck, Hygiene #4 in action. **POWER Opposes (P3) ACQUITTED** — sign flips +1.36 to -0.70/ep. **Late Climax (3d) = the single surviving leak suspect**: positive on BOTH windows (+4.80/ep 3d n=3; +3.63/ep 5d n=5), beats GO control per-episode on both (+1.89 / +0.91) — but below the 8-EP bar, so `[8n]` stays ARMED, not fired; trigger = >=8 Climax EP on both windows (loop adds ~1/day; a `--days 10` tape checks sooner). **Proven-right guards, both windows:** Family Gate (-0.37/ep 3d, -1.40/ep 5d over 268 EP 5/5 days; avoided -60.4 -> -375.1), Session Dead-Zone (-3.36 / -4.29), Conf<35% (-4.47 both, same 4 EP), Conf<50 3b + SNIPER AUTH (negative both). GO control 5d: +8.2 pts / 9 EP / 4-of-5 days; `[8c]` ruler N=36 fixed-wave -2.1 = anchor tie (tape verified line-by-line: `[8m]` cyc totals tie `[2]` numerators; GO 36 = `[8c]` N=36). **Owner order (`2026-11-11`) stands: Step 2e HANDS fix — near-reach TP never moved further away + SL laws + `exit_report_log()` as ONE exit stage — runs next, replay-first, both windows, live untouched until it wins and owner says yes.**

**STEP 2e EXHIBIT A — the Hands' own diary, 2026-11-11 demo (first live capture; `data/management_log.csv`):** both GO trades were SELLS (audit BUY rows = the closing deals), entry ~4017.33 and ~4003.52, 0.01 lot. They peaked at **+$15.84 / +$15.43** (max 31.27) and closed at **+$9.61 / +$11.55** (day +$21.16) — the Hands taxed **32% of the fish**. The owner's real-case memory IS trade #1, to the minute: at 15:32:11 price 4001.49 had ALREADY swum through the original TP 4003.70 (~$13.6 win, missed at 0.9 pts on a 1-min sample grid) — worse, at 15:23:12 `TP_UPDATE` had pulled TP 4003.70->4000.59 while price was 7 pts above, and at 15:32 CHANDELIER pulled it to 3986.91 (30.4 pts = the owner's "$30", reason *TP front-runs fresh opposing structure*) then moved it back to 4000.67 at 15:33 — bucket ping-pong on a <1-minute blip; 15:34:39 a 6.2-pt breath hit the raised SL 4007.68 (+9.61). Diary census for the day: `TP_UPDATE` moved TP away 7x (up to 13.7 pts); `BE` fired early at +0.77R/+0.98R (*"early: the move lacks CVD support"*); `WALL_SHELTER`+`CHANDELIER_WAVE_TRAIL` tightened the same SL from two directions to a 3.8-6.2 pt standoff = smaller than one normal wave-breath. Code-verified 2026-11-12: tournament meta-rule = **tightest proposal wins every cycle** (`cands` in position_manager); live BE triggers = PM_BE_TRIGGER_R 0.75R (0.5R flow-weak), wave 0.75R both modes; BE level = entry + max(0.1xATR, spread) buffer. **Step 2e LAWS (owner order, built on this exhibit): (1) BUCKET RATCHET - TP may only move toward price, never away; (2) ONE KNOT OWNER - exactly one rule per cycle may tighten SL, standoff >= 1.5xATR, only on a new high-water; (3) BE only at 2R - the early CVD-jump is deleted; (4) every candidate = preview row on the ruler, must beat fixed-wave 2.0/3.5 on 3d AND 5d; (5) `exit_report_log()` folds INTO `management_log.csv` and extends to all five Doors - no second diary.**

**STEP 2e CANDIDATE #1 — "LADDER-4" (OWNER DESIGN, `2026-11-12`):** 4 imagined TP rungs between entry and the fixed wave target (proposal: 0.75R / 1.5R / 2.25R / 3R). TP line NEVER moves (promise kept). The SL standoff behind high-water is set by the LAST RUNG price has crossed: the further price runs, the smaller the gap -> standoff 1.5x / 1.25x / 1.0x / 1.0x ATR — with a HARD FLOOR >= 1.0xATR (today's disease was standoffs below one breath; a normal wave-breath measures 1.2-1.5xATR, so a "tight at top" variant must earn it on tape, not by logic). SL moves ONLY on rung crossings (max 4 per trade, confirmed on closed M3 bar — the per-minute most-nervous-rule-wins tournament is gone); each crossing also RAISES the locked floor (ratchet: SL never loosens). Rung-1 replaces the current early BE jump (0.75R/0.5R code-verified above). Scored as ruler preview rows: LADDER-4 (floor 1.0xATR) vs LADDER-4-WIDE (floor 1.5x) vs champion fixed-wave;
**LADDER-4+2LOCK (owner refinement, same night): the entry's initial SL stays EXACTLY as the entry engine sets it (it defines R and keeps tape comparability with the 2.0/3.5 anchor); TP fixed at the wave target; the ladder uses the 4 rungs only as EVENTS (confirmed on closed M3 bar) and moves the SL at most TWICE per trade -> rung 2 crossed (50% of TP distance): SL to break-even (+spread buffer); rung 3 crossed (75%): SL to +0.5R profit-lock; rung 4 = the fixed TP itself closes the trade - so NO HWM chandelier trail is needed at all, because with a fixed promise the trade always dies at TP or at a lock, never mid-wave. Max 2 SL moves vs today's 14-move nervousness tournament. Variant list for the ruler now: LADDER-4 (floor 1.0xATR) / LADDER-4-WIDE (floor 1.5x) / LADDER-4+2LOCK (minimal) / champion fixed-wave - bar unchanged: beat the champion on 3d AND 5d, then owner yes.** owner's yes required before anything touches the boat. Design principle accepted from owner: **keep the old system, change only WHO may move WHICH line and WHEN** — minimum diff, maximum measurement.

**OWNER-LADDER v2 — the owner's clarification, `2026-11-12`, THE shape Step 2e builds:** initial SL at entry stays EXACTLY as the entry engine sets it (defines R, keeps tape comparability). The SL then climbs in FOUR owned steps, ratchet-only (never loosens), events counted ONLY on a closed M3 bar: (1) at **+0.75R** move SL only HALF the distance toward entry (risk cut 50%, but the trade keeps ~1.25R of breathing room — this is the fix for the 0.75R full-teleport disease, and it is BETTER than the "BE at 2R" of law #3 because it de-risks early without dying on normal breaths); (2) at **+1.0R** move SL to entry + buffer, buffer = max(1.5x spread, 0.1xATR) + per-lot commission converted to pts ("risk-free" that is honest about fees); (3) at **rung 3** (75% of the fixed TP distance) lock +0.5R; (4) **rung 4 = the fixed TP line** - price lands, trade closes at full promise (TP itself NEVER moves - law 1). No HWM chandelier anywhere: with a fixed promise, the trade dies at TP or at a lock, never mid-wave. Max 3 SL moves per trade vs Tuesday's 14-move tournament. Ruler variant name: `LADDER-OWNER-v2`; rivals: LADDER-4-WIDE / plain fixed-wave champion; bar: win on 3d AND 5d (then 10d), then owner yes. Re-check on the two Tuesday fish under these exact rules: #1 survives the 6.2pt breath (half-step leaves SL far above) and LANDS at fixed TP 4003.70 = +13.6 vs actual +9.61; #2 lands at TP = +10.1 vs actual +11.55 (trail squeezed 1.5 more that day) - net +23.7 vs +21.16 on 2 fish; the tape decides whether the net holds across 36+.

**STEP 2e GEOMETRY RECIPE — DOOR vs RUNGS (owner Q&A `2026-11-12`, code-verified same day):** opening geometry as it actually works in `shooting_team.py`: TP line = the nearest FRONT door/wall moved back by buffer = max(2xspread, 0.15xATR); SL line = shelter wall behind, stop = shelter +/- same buffer (`BOOK_SHELTER`), accepted ONLY if risk is within [1.5, 2.5]xATR and on the safe side, else `ATR_FALLBACK` = entry + 2.0xATR; every receipt stamps stop_source. Tuesday demonstrated both branches live: fish #1 SL 7.55 pts = 2.0xATR fallback; fish #2 SL 5.77 = wall shelter at the 1.5x lower bound. **Ladder clarification for the future (do not re-derive):** the 4 TP lines are NOT four doors - they are the single entry->door distance quartered at opening, imaginary chalk checkpoints that ONLY drive SL steps; rung 4 IS the original TP promise, so the win condition is IDENTICAL to the old system and nothing deeper must be reached. Old `TP_UPDATE` behavior was door-chasing: re-aim the bucket at whatever new wall appeared (Tuesday: 4003.70->4000.59->3986.91) - the owner's words "should go so much far to reach all the doors, which is not happening never" correctly describe THAT disease; law #1 freezes the door forever. **Five-year-old recap:** the robot reads one small pond and guesses where the fish jump. It guesses wrong often, and when the water looks flat it freezes (“NEITHER”). Next we look at TWO more ponds — the big pond (are the fish swimming our way? any rocks ahead?) and the tiny pond (how big is one wave?) — then we write the rules DOWN, score them in the practice room against the old guesser, and only if it wins does it get on the boat.

## Measurement Hygiene LAWS (`2026-11-11`, born from Step 2 + Step 2d the same day — standing)

1. **Measure before you code.** One pinned replay day kills a whole design before it reaches the boat — two losers prevented this way (R-multiple doors, direction-referee).
2. **Both-window rule.** Any cohort score (gate or exit proposal) is reported on the pinned 3-day AND 5-day windows and must win on BOTH; absolute numbers are window-scoped, never compared across day-sets (same tool: +39.1 on 3d vs -2.1 on 5d).
3. **Per-cycle bucket numbers are HINTS, not verdicts** — a flag held across minutes double-counts; money claims require episode-level dedup (`[8g]` style) before any gate change is proposed.
4. **Thin-cell law: n<8 = direction hint, never a conclusion** — printed inside every grid so a reader cannot miss it.
5. **Size in the sea's units, never in our fear's units** — doors/fences in ATR of the SAME TF as the plan; no door trigger beyond own TP; no BE teleport inside 1xATR of entry (Step 2 autopsy).
6. **Identical old rows = proof of tool honesty.** A measure-only change must leave every pre-existing section bit-identical; if not, the tool (not the market) is guilty first.
7. **Revert failing experiments immediately; commit only judge-side files; state LIVE vs NOT-LIVE explicitly.** An uncommitted failed patch on disk is a loaded gun aimed at the next accidental restart.
8. **Delivery addendum:** the viewer DISPLAYS, it does not transfer — patches must physically land in `Downloads` and be `wc -l` + `sha256sum` verified BEFORE `git apply`; ship ONE patch computed from the rig's CURRENT exact blob, never dependent chains that can half-fail.
9. **Anchors are read, never recalled.** This very update: a script that edited by remembered strings silently no-oped two register lines (caught by post-write grep re-read — the Book's own #1 law applies to the Book itself).

### STEP 2f PRE-REGISTRATION — THE ELASTIC ROPE (`2026-11-12`, written BEFORE the tape; owner's own design)
**The idea:** keep the SL line trailing the peak, but the gap is not constant - it shrinks the further price has run from entry (more profit earned = less slack allowed). Smooth (no rungs), ratchet-only (never loosens), TP frozen at the wave (Law #1).
**Arm in the ruler (tool blob `a8925e51`, row `Step 2f Preview: ELASTIC TRAIL on FIXED WAVE`):** gap = max(0.5 x opening-R, (1.5 - 0.25 x peak_R) x ATR_run); moves decided on closed-M3 peaks; exits against lines valid at bar start (SL before TP, no intra-bar peek); universe = the champion's own fix_out-eligible cycles with sl_fix/tp_fix + run-constant ATR - exactly the ladder arm's geometry, so row-vs-Benchmark is one-variable fair.
**The bar, fixed now, no re-tuning EVER:** on BOTH pinned seas (`--dates 2026-11-03,2026-11-04,2026-11-05,2026-11-06,2026-11-09` and `--dates 2026-11-09,2026-11-10,2026-11-11`) the elastic row must beat `Benchmark: 2.0x SL / 3.5x TP Fixed Wave` (-2.1 A / +82.0 B). The ladder row stays printed as the archive reference. **Win both** -> live arm proposal may be drafted (behind flag, with `exit_report_log()` finally built to justify it). **Lose either** -> rope-tightening retires with the ladder: this engine's exit = anchor well + hands off, no further rope chapters on borrowed seas.
**Prediction on record (made before, so it can be caught):** loses sea A (chop eats slack); on sea B it may claw near the champion but not past it - "close" is not "beats", and only beats ships.

## STRATEGY (open ledger - ordered, append-only; started `2026-11-12`, owner instruction: keep every strategy conversation HERE, in THIS order; re-visit after the big system change, together with the STEP 2g checkpoint)
**Order of study, fixed by owner (do not reorder):** 1) Auction, Imbalance, Balance. 2) Volume Profile, VAH, VAL, POC, session VWAP + anchored VWAP, resistance/support lines. 3) Delta, Absorption, Exhaustion in order flow. Sources consulted for these definitions: the Market Profile/AMT tradition (Steidlmayer, CBOT 1980s) via current practitioner references (luxalgo, algostorm, tradeology, tra-mada order-flow/volume-profile guides, united-daytraders absorption/exhaustion study) - cross-checked against each other, no single-source claims kept.

### Entry 1 - AUCTION / BALANCE / IMBALANCE (`2026-11-12`)
The market is a two-way auction whose job is to ADVERTISE price until business gets done. Where time+volume pile up, price is ACCEPTED (= value). Where price pokes and nobody trades there, it is REJECTED. BALANCE = both sides agree: price rotates, profile is round/bell (D-shaped), value areas of consecutive sessions OVERLAP, point of control crossed freely, VWAP flat. Roughly 70% of session time per CME-data studies - the memory only keeps the 30%. IMBALANCE = one side aggressive enough to force the auction to SEARCH for new value: elongated profile, one-timeframing (a level touched only once, never traded again that session), value MIGRATING day over day, VWAP sloping with price, range extension backed by strong relative volume and directional delta. Acceptance vs failure test: after leaving the old value area, do time AND volume build outside it (new value forming -> run continues) or does it wick back inside (probe failed -> rotation back to old POC). **Vocabulary collision, law:** the classroom's 3/6/9/12 "imbalance" is a BAR-level footprint diagonal; AMT "imbalance" is a DAY-state. In this book say `bar-imbalance` vs `day-imbalance`, never just "imbalance". For our engine: `Regime(TREND/RANGE/flat_lake/vr_chop)` is already a day-state detector - AMT is the theory behind it, and it sits ABOVE every setup (the profitable behaviors in the two states are near opposites).

### Entry 2 - THE TOOLKIT: VOLUME PROFILE / VAH / VAL / POC / VWAPs / S-R (`2026-11-12`)
**Volume Profile** = the histogram laid sideways: volume traded AT EACH PRICE (not per time - that is what makes it an auction transcript). **POC** = the single price with the most volume: the fairest price, behaves as magnet + support/resistance. **Value Area (VA)** = the contiguous band holding ~70% of the period's volume (70% = one std-dev 68.2% rounded by Steidlmayer), built centered on POC; **VAH/VAL** = its upper/lower edge = primary fade levels in balance and the doorway for initiative in imbalance: above VAH WITH volume = initiative buying, above VAH on thin volume = probe to fade. **VWAP** = volume-weighted average price: the running live consensus (dynamic fair value); in balance price rotates around a flat VWAP, a VWAP sloping away with price is the average's vote that imbalance took over. **"WVAP1/VWAP2" decoded for the owner:** session VWAP (starts at day open) and ANCHORED VWAP (starts at any event you choose: news, swing low, yesterday's POC) - many platforms list them as two tools plus deviation bands; we own `vwap_c` live already. **SUPPORT/RESISTANCE, auction flavor:** not drawn lines but acceptance facts - HVNs (volume hills) defend, LVNs (valleys) get traversed FAST again because the market already said "not fair here"; walls/doors are our version. Already in the boat: memory_doors = POC/VA/HTF_POC/OB feed the TP ladder (`shooting_team.py` step 3), `PM_HTF_POC_ENABLE=1`, and scout `Door Reach & Hold` ([8e]: reached 41-43%, held 37-45%) is literally measuring acceptance vs rejection per door.

### Entry 3 - ORDER FLOW: DELTA / ABSORPTION / EXHAUSTION (`2026-11-12`)
Every trade has an aggressor: market orders CROSS the spread and move price; limit orders WAIT and soak. **DELTA** = aggressive-buy volume minus aggressive-sell volume inside one bar; **CVD** = the running total (session aggression ledger). **Delta divergence** = price prints a new high while delta fades/negative (buyer fatigue) or new low while delta strengthens (seller exhaustion). **ABSORPTION** = heavy aggression + NO price movement - a passive defender is taking everything offered (big volume at one level, price locked in a tight range): aggression meeting a WALL. **EXHAUSTION** = aggression drying up: each push prints thinner at the extreme, delta shrinks wave over wave, candle closes off its high: a TIRED ENGINE. A selloff can stop because someone catches it (absorption) or because nobody is left to throw (exhaustion) - both end moves, only absorption says there is a buyer underneath. One bar-imbalance is noise; stacked bar-imbalances + absorption/exhaustion AT a key level (VWAP/VAH/VAL/P-previous-H-L) is the signal; absorption at the extreme is often the pair-mate (absorbed then giving up = highest-quality reversal). **Boat map:** the melting ladder IS an absorption/exhaustion machine on the defenders' side - `solid/eroding/melting/melting-fast` = aggression eating a wall, with `melting-fast` = wall about to exhaust; power sensors `cvd_momentum` (delta trend), `l3_aggr_limit` (aggression vs resting limit = absorption candidate), `footprint_levels/big_prints` exist but are NOT yet promoted into any gate ([8f] row 4: cvd_momentum 47.6% WR -0.24 - weak AS SENSOR, untested AS CONTEXT).
**Strategy research agenda (measure-first, laws stay armed):** (a) replay [8e] split by day-state (balance vs imbalance): does door HOLD only mean something in balance? (b) absorption signature (high burn-rate with price NOT breaking) as a door-quality feature vs current reach/hold; (c) CVD slope at signal time as gate CONTEXT (divergence guard), never as a new threshold without [8n]-style episodes; (d) anchored-VWAP at yesterday's POC as the memory-door candidate the TP ladder should prefer; (e) all four get bars written BEFORE any live gate moves. ORDER: the three entries above stand as the shared vocabulary for the coming big system change; when it lands, this ledger gets a new numbered entry and the STEP 2f/2g open files are re-graded on fresh tapes.
